Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the nucleus space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 142 and the convergence rate is O(435/n^1).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 142 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the nucleus structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 435. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
